# Gap table: wader

Made by `tools/views/gaps.py`. The 852 species most recorded worldwide that borrow this view cover 100.0% of its 3,172,323 research-grade records; the 20 most recorded in Alachua County cover 96.0% of the county's 12,583. Counts are iNaturalist research-grade records through 2024. The view's own measured animal is not excluded: it shows as `m` where it was measured.

Row grades: `m` every trait of the row measured in that species; `g` every trait measured or carried by a rule; `i` at least one trait neither. The detail columns give the state of each trait.

Rows and their traits:

- **Head movement**: eye_movement, fov_h_deg
- **Speed**: cff_hz
- **Sharpness**: acuity_cpd, interreceptor_deg
- **Ultraviolet**: lambda_max_nm, spectral_range_nm
- **Field of view**: fov_h_deg, binocular_deg
